213	/** Main.buffNoSave 全名单（Main.cs:8896-8990：不写入玩家存档的 buff;
214	 *  含 173-181 整段 for 循环）——本仓仅存 BUFF_DEFS 已映射项,交集生效 */
215	const BUFF_NO_SAVE = new Set([
216	  20, 22, 23, 24, 28, 29, 30, 31, 34, 35, 37, 38, 39, 43, 44, 46, 47, 48,
217	  58, 59, 60, 62, 63, 64, 67, 68, 69, 70, 72, 80, 87, 88, 89, 93, 94, 95,
218	  96, 97, 98, 99, 100, 103, 119, 120, 125, 126, 133, 134, 135, 137, 139,
219	  140, 144, 146, 147, 150, 158, 159, 161, 163, 164, 170, 171, 172, 173, 174,
220	  175, 176, 177, 178, 179, 180, 181, 182, 187, 188, 194, 195, 196, 197, 198,
221	  199, 205, 213, 214, 215, 263, 271, 320, 321, 322, 325, 335, 348, 353, 355,
222	  366, 385, 386,
223	]);
224	/** vanilla buff id → 本仓 BuffType 反查（存档恢复用） */
225	const BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA = new Map<number, BuffType>(
226	  (Object.keys(BUFF_DEFS) as unknown as BuffType[]).map((t) => [BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff, t]),
227	);
228	
229	/** Buff 名:原版 BuffName.<Internal>(Lang 未就绪回退中文兜底) */
230	export function buffName(t: BuffType): string {
231	  return Lang.buffName(BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff) ?? BUFF_DEFS[t].fallbackName;
232	}
233	
234	/** Buff 描述:自有键(机制数值是本仓库实现,不用原版通用描述) */
235	export function buffDesc(t: BuffType): string {
236	  return Lang.text(BUFF_DEFS[t].descKey);
237	}
238	
239	/** HP/s 汇总表（UpdateBuffs lifeRegen 项换算：lifeRegen N = N/2 HP/s） */
240	const HP_PER_SEC: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
241	  [BuffType.Regen]: 2,   // lifeRegen+4
242	  [BuffType.Honey]: 1,   // lifeRegen+2
243	};
244	
245	/** DoT 分块大小（Player.cs:19136-19204 结算 + HurtLifeRegen:19207）：burned/
246	 *  suffocating 走 -600 阈值分支 → 每 600 life-regen 点弹 HurtLifeRegen(5)；
247	 *  其余（OnFire 等）走通用 -120 分支 → 每 120 点弹 1。换算到时间：
248	 *  Burning(−60/t)=每 10t 弹 5（≈3 次/秒飘字）、Suffocation(−40/t)=每 15t 弹 5、
249	 *  OnFire(−8/t)=每 15t 弹 1。掉血须整块弹出并伴随 LifeRegen 色(255,60,70)
250	 *  dot 飘字（CombatText.cs:24 / NewText dot:true），非每帧静默 1 HP 滴流 */
251	const DOT_CHUNK: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
252	  [BuffType.Burning]: 5,
253	  [BuffType.Suffocation]: 5,
254	};
255	
256	/** 环境 DoT 表（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911 的 onFire→burned→suffocating 顺序，
257	 *  速率 = lifeRegen 负项 / 2 = HP/s）。每个 DoT 结算前先清零正 lifeRegen——
258	 *  恢复类 buff 不能抵消 DoT（tick 内消费） */
259	const ENV_DOTS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [BuffType, number]> = [
260	  [BuffType.OnFire, -4],      // lifeRegen-8（:18793，vampireSeed 分支未移植）
261	  [BuffType.Burning, -30],    // lifeRegen-60（:18862，另 moveSpeed×0.5 在 moveSpeedMult）
262	  [BuffType.Suffocation, -20], // lifeRegen-40（:18889）
263	];
264	
265	export class BuffState {
266	  active = new Map<BuffType, number>(); // 剩余秒（≈buffTime/60）
267	  campfireAccum = 0;
268	  /** 狼人回复独立累加器（:11496 lifeRegen++ 恒 0.5 HP/s，与篝火强度/节拍无关） */
269	  werewolfAccum = 0;
270	  lanternAccum = 0;
271	  /** 当前篝火光环强度(Game 每 20tick 扫描写入:篝火+1,心灯额外+1) */
272	  campfireHeal = 1;
273	  /** 心灯(lifeRegen+2 = 1 HP/s,Player.cs:12247):Game 扫描写入 0/1 */
274	  lanternHeal = 0;
275	  /** AddBuff 语义（Player.cs:5138-5150）：同类取 max 不缩短——浸入类 buff 可每帧无脑续期 */
276	  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
277	    const cur = this.active.get(type);
278	    if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
279	  }
280	  has(type: BuffType): boolean {
281	    return this.active.has(type);
282	  }
283	  remove(type: BuffType) {
284	    this.active.delete(type);
285	  }
286	  /** 直写剩余秒（原版 `buffTime[i] = n` 赋值语义，可缩短——区别于 apply 的
287	   *  AddBuff max 合并）。消费方：玩家微光穿落 buffTime=6t 刷新（Player.cs:11416） */
288	  set(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
289	    this.active.set(type, durationSec);
290	  }
291	  /** 存档序列化（Player.cs:53913-53925 buffType+buffTime 槽位语义）:
292	   *  存 vanilla id + 剩余秒;Main.buffNoSave 名单(Main.cs:8896-8990)过滤不落盘的 buff */
293	  toSave(): Array<[number, number]> {
294	    const out: Array<[number, number]> = [];
295	    for (const [t, sec] of this.active) {
296	      const v = BUFF_DEFS[t]?.vanillaBuff;
297	      if (v === undefined || BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
298	      out.push([v, Math.max(1, Math.round(sec))]);
299	    }
300	    return out;
301	  }
302	  /** 读档恢复（旧档/导入缺省=无 buff;未知 vanilla id 静默丢弃——后续接入的 buff 不丢档） */
303	  applySaved(list: Array<[number, number]> | undefined): void {
304	    if (!list) return;
305	    for (const [v, sec] of list) {
306	      if (BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
307	      const t = BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA.get(v);
308	      if (t !== undefined) this.active.set(t, sec);
309	    }
310	  }
311	  /** 本帧弹出的 DoT 分块伤害合计（HurtLifeRegen 语义,Player 消费后弹出飘字并清零） */
312	  private dotChunkOut = 0;
313	  consumeDotChunk(): number {
314	    const c = this.dotChunkOut;
315	    this.dotChunkOut = 0;
316	    return c;
317	  }
318	  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */
319	  tick(dt: number): number {
320	    let heal = 0;
321	    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {
322	      const left = time - dt;
323	      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);
324	      else this.active.set(t, left);
325	    }
326	    // ---- UpdateBuffs 正项（Player.cs:9640+）：Regeneration lifeRegen+=4（2 HP/s）、
327	    //      Honey :18955 lifeRegen+=2（1 HP/s）、狼人 :11496 lifeRegen+=1（0.5 HP/s）。
328	    //      注意原版这些都在 NaturalLifeRegen 的 DoT 段（:18779+）**之前**求值——
329	    //      DoT 的 if(lifeRegen>0) lifeRegen=0 会把它们整池清零（见下）----
330	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
331	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
332	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) {
333	      // 狼人 lifeRegen++（:11496）= 0.5 HP/s → 每 2 秒恒 1 HP（独立累加器，勿与篝火
334	      // 共用 campfireAccum/campfireHeal——曾误挂致篝火不在时狼人回复随强度归零/错峰）
335	      this.werewolfAccum += dt;
336	      if (this.werewolfAccum >= 2) { this.werewolfAccum -= 2; heal += 1; }
337	    }
338	    // ---- DoT 段（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911）：每个 DoT 先 if(lifeRegen>0)
339	    //      lifeRegen=0 再累加负项——恢复类 buff **不能**抵消 OnFire/Burning 等
340	    //      （曾直接相加，恢复池被 DoT 按净额吃掉）。分块弹出（DOT_CHUNK），
341	    //      净伤害记入 dotChunkOut 供 HurtLifeRegen 飘字消费 ----
342	    let dots = 0;
343	    for (const [t, hpPerSec] of ENV_DOTS) {
344	      if (!this.active.has(t)) continue;
345	      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;                       // :18781 等 if (lifeRegen > 0) lifeRegen = 0
346	      const d = this.rateAccum(t, hpPerSec, dt);
347	      dots += d;
348	      heal += d;
349	    }
350	    if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;
351	    // 蜂蜜对冲（Player.cs:18915-18922：lifeRegen<0 时 honey 额外 lifeRegen+4 = +2 HP/s，
352	    // 且对冲后钳回 ≤0）——DoT 系统已实装,补上 buff-system-port 备忘里标"暂缺"的分支
353	    if (heal < 0 && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {
354	      heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);
355	      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;
356	    }
357	    // ---- 光环正项（NaturalLifeRegen :18988-18993，位于 DoT 段之后——篝火/心灯
358	    //      的 lifeRegen 按原版可对冲 DoT）----
359	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Campfire)) {
360	      // 原版 lifeRegen+1 = 0.5 HP/s(Player.cs:18990/12244-12245)→ 每 2 秒 1 HP
361	      this.campfireAccum += dt;
362	      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) {
363	        this.campfireAccum -= 2;
364	        heal += this.campfireHeal;
365	      }
366	    }
367	    if (this.lanternHeal > 0) {
368	      this.lanternAccum += dt;
369	      if (this.lanternAccum >= 1) {
370	        this.lanternAccum -= 1;
371	        heal += this.lanternHeal;
372	      }
373	    }
374	    return heal;
375	  }
376	  /** 定额回复/伤害 buff 的独立累加器（Map 存每 buff 的秒余数;负速率=DoT）。
377	   *  separate=true 用独立累加器键(蜂蜜对冲与常规蜂蜜回复分账,避免共用余数)。
378	   *  负速率按 DOT_CHUNK 整块弹出（≈原版 lifeRegenCount 阈值分块）。已知偏差:
379	   *  原版 lifeRegenCount 是全局共享计数器,buff 到期后负余量仍残留(由后续
380	   *  DoT/自然回复对冲);本实现 per-buff 累加器到期即弃余数(<1 块,≤4 HP,一次性) */
381	  private rateLeft = new Map<string, number>();
382	  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
383	    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
384	    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
385	    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
386	    let heal = 0;
387	    if (hpPerSec >= 0) {
388	      while (left >= 1) { left -= 1; heal += 1; }
389	    } else {
390	      const chunk = DOT_CHUNK[t] ?? 1;
391	      // 原版 lifeRegenCount 是整数累加（-600/-120 阈值精确）;此处 dt 浮点积分
392	      // 在块边界会差 ~1e-15（如 1/3×15=4.999…）,补 epsilon 保证阈值时点对齐
393	      while (left <= -chunk + 1e-9) { left += chunk; heal -= chunk; }
394	    }
395	    this.rateLeft.set(key, left);
396	    return heal;
397	  }
398	  // ---- 属性聚合（幂等读取，不做副作用写入）----
399	  get moveSpeedMult(): number {
400	    let m = this.active.has(BuffType.Agility) ? 1.25 : 1;   // Swiftness moveSpeed += 0.25
401	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Burning)) m *= 0.5;         // Burning moveSpeed *= 0.5（:18886）
402	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Chilled)) m *= 0.75;        // Chilled moveSpeed *= 0.75（:25659-25661）
403	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Sunflower)) m *= 1.21;      // 向日葵 +0.1 再 ×1.1 两步复合（:10598）
404	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Weak)) m *= 0.9;            // 虚弱 moveSpeed -= 0.1（:11510）
405	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) m *= 1.05;      // 狼人 moveSpeed += 0.05（:11498）
406	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.KiteBuff)) m += 0.25;       // 风筝 moveSpeed += 0.25（:9614）
407	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.SugarRush)) m *= 1.2;       // 糖分冲刺 moveSpeed += 0.2（:9634）
408	    m += [0, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4][this.wellFedTier];               // 食物 moveSpeed += 0.2/0.3/0.4（:11532+）
409	    return m;
410	  }
411	  get defenseBonus(): number {
412	    let d = this.active.has(BuffType.Ironskin) ? 8 : 0;     // Ironskin statDefense += 8（1456）
413	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.CatBast)) d += 5;          // 猫堡垒 statDefense += 5（:9778）
414	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Tipsy)) d -= 4;            // 醉酒 statDefense -= 4（:11514）
415	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Weak)) d -= 4;              // 虚弱 statDefense -= 4（:11509）
416	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) d += 3;         // 狼人 statDefense += 3（:11497）
417	    d += [0, 2, 3, 4][this.wellFedTier];                    // 食物 statDefense += 2/3/4（:11524+）
418	    return d;
419	  }

432	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed3)) return 3;
433	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed2)) return 2;
434	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed)) return 1;

    // 507 星云仅环境粒子（:41287-41309），怪全靠 Zone 刷怪表——略

    // noTileCollide + noGravity：直接位移（原版速度由引擎积分）
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    this.onGround = false;
  }

  /** 受击。返回是否死亡 */
  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, kby: number, game: GameHooks): boolean {
    // Critter 兼容：critters 桶的调用方按 Critter.hurt(game) 单参调用；
    // 原版小动物是 Enemy 进该桶，这里把 Game 对象重映射回 game 参、伤害取固定 5
    if (typeof dmg === 'object' && dmg !== null) {
      game = dmg as unknown as GameHooks;
      dmg = 5; kbx = 0; kby = 0;
    }
    const rawDmg = dmg; // 傀儡上报用原始值（下方 def 凘认算只做一次，双端一致）
    if (this.iframes > 0) return false;
    if (this.dontTakeDamage) return false; // 原版 NPC.dontTakeDamage（StrikeNPC 早退）
    this.justHitT = 2; // 原版 justHit 单帧旗标（AI_023 飞行武器/陆龟受击回态消费）
    // 训练假人（NPC 488，cs:83498 localAI 赋值）：immortal 永不死，
    // 受击只记摇晃强度+方向（渲染帧端消费），掉血由 dummyAI 回满
    if (this.vanillaId === 488) {
      this.dummyShake = Math.max(20, Math.min(120, dmg));
      this.dummyHitDir = Math.sign(kbx) || this.dummyHitDir;
      this.iframes = 8;
      return true;
    }
    // 猪鲨爆裂泡泡（NPC 371，StrikeNPC cs:82102-82107）：伤害清零(num=0)不扣血，
    // 直接转入爆炸态 ai[0]=1/ai[1]=4 + dontTakeDamage——"被击即爆"而非"被打死"；
    // aiStyle 70 (:35180-35194) 据此扩 100×100 并 EncourageDespawn(3) 消散
    if (this.vanillaId === 371) {
      this.ai0 = 1; this.ai1 = 4;
      this.iframes = 8; // dontTakeDamage（爆炸 4t 内不可再受击）
      return true;
    }
    // 原版 Main.CalculateDamageNPCsTake：dmg - defense*0.5，下限 1
    const def = this.def.defense ?? 0;
    // ichor（灵液药剂）：NPC.checkArmorPenetration（NPC.cs:81913-81938）——
    // armorPenetration += 15，结算 (armorPenetration/2) 整除 = +7 直伤；
    // 超防钳制（def>2×dmg 时按 dmg 上限，防穿透把伤害推到全额以上）
    const pen = this.ichorT > 0 ? 7 : 0;
    const effDef = def > dmg * 2 ? dmg * 2 : def;
    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - effDef * 0.5 + pen));
    // takenDamageMultiplier（NPC.StrikeNPC cs:81997-82000）：防御结算后 >1 时 ×N
    // （419 旋刃怪冲刺中 ×3，cs:57172）
    if (this.takenDamageMultiplier > 1) dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * this.takenDamageMultiplier));
    this.hp -= dmg;
    this.iframes = 8;
    this.hpBarT = 240; // 受击后血条持续显示 4 秒
    const resist = this.def.knockbackResist;
    if (resist < 0.9) {
      this.vx += kbx * (1 - resist);
      this.vy += kby * (1 - resist);
    }
    // 联机傀儡受击：上报房主权威结算（本地视觉反馈已生效：扣血/飘字/击退）
    if (this.netPuppet) entityNet.reportStrike?.(this.netId, rawDmg, kbx, kby);
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      // 月总族(396/397/398)血尽不直死:原版 checkDead(:78841-78867)转为闭眼残肢/死亡演出,
      // 转换逻辑在各自 AI 首行(hp<=0 → ai0 置态+满血续命),此处放行让 AI 下一 tick 接管
      if (this.vanillaId === 396 || this.vanillaId === 397 || this.vanillaId === 398) return false;
      // 石巨人族(245/246/247/248):血尽转换在各 AI 首行(本体死亡演出/头转自由态),同样放行
      if (this.vanillaId === 245 || this.vanillaId === 246 || this.vanillaId === 247 || this.vanillaId === 248) return false;
      // 星璇四塔(517/422/507/493)：checkDead（NPC.cs:78867-78875）血尽转 ai2=1 死亡演出
      // （ai1=0、满血续命 + dontTakeDamage），180t 上升渐隐后由 towerAI 走标准死亡管线（掉碎片）
      if (this.vanillaId != null && this.vanillaId in TOWER_KEY && this.ai2 !== 1) {
        this.ai2 = 1; this.ai1 = 0; this.hp = this.maxHp; this.iframes = 2;
        return false;
      }
      this.dead = true;
      // 瓢虫被杀 → 坏运（NPC.checkDead cs:78838-78840 → LadyBugKilled :82332）：
      // 604 普通 / 605 金瓢虫（距离衰减公式见 LuckState.ladyBugKilled）；
      // 副产物 ladyBugRainBoost +1800 钳 18000（cs:82334-82338，雨调度提前开雨）
      if (this.vanillaId === 604 || this.vanillaId === 605) {
        const pl = (game as unknown as { player?: Player }).player;
        if (pl && !pl.dead) {
          pl.luckState?.ladyBugKilled(Math.hypot(this.cx - pl.cx, this.cy - pl.cy), this.vanillaId === 605);
        }
        const w = game.world.weather;
        w.ladyBugRainBoost = Math.min(18000, w.ladyBugRainBoost + 1800);
      }

	public int checkArmorPenetration(int armorPenetration, float armorPenetrationPercent)
	{
		if (ichor)
		{
			armorPenetration += 15;
		}
		if (brokenArmor)
		{
			armorPenetration += 20;
		}
		if (betsysCurse)
		{
			armorPenetration += 40;
		}
		armorPenetration += (int)((float)defense * armorPenetrationPercent);
		if (armorPenetration <= 0 || defense <= 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		if (armorPenetration > defense)
		{
			return defense / 2;
		}
		return armorPenetration / 2;
	}

	public double StrikeNPCNoInteraction(int Damage, float knockBack, int hitDirection, bool crit = false, bool noEffect = false, bool fromNet = false)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 0)
		{
			ignorePlayerInteractions++;
		}
		return StrikeNPC(Damage, knockBack, hitDirection, crit, noEffect, fromNet, 255);
	}

	public int KeyBrandStrike(int plr, int dmg, Vector2 bestSwordSpot)
	{
		float t = (float)life / (float)lifeMax;
		float lerpValue = Utils.GetLerpValue(1f, 0.1f, t, clamped: true);
		float num = 1f * lerpValue;
		dmg = (int)((float)dmg * (1f + num));
		Vector2 positionInWorld = base.Hitbox.ClosestPointInRect(bestSwordSpot);
		ParticleOrchestrator.RequestParticleSpawn(clientOnly: false, ParticleOrchestraType.Keybrand, new ParticleOrchestraSettings
		{
			PositionInWorld = positionInWorld
		}, plr);
		return dmg;
	}

	public double StrikeNPC(int Damage, float knockBack, int hitDirection, bool crit = false, bool noEffect = false, bool fromNet = false, int owner = -1)
	{
		if (owner < 0)
		{
			owner = Main.myPlayer;
		}

Very important finding! `checkArmorPenetration` (NPC.cs:81913-81938):
- ichor → armorPenetration += 15
- brokenArmor → armorPenetration += 20
- betsysCurse → armorPenetration += 40
- Then `armorPenetration += (int)(defense * armorPenetrationPercent)`
- If armorPenetration <= 0 || defense <= 0 → return 0
- If armorPenetration > defense → return defense / 2
- else return armorPenetration / 2

So the returned value is used as a damage bonus (damage += checkArmorPenetration(...)). With ichor (+15) and defense, e.g. defense=20: 15+? no percent → 15. 15 <= 20 → returns 7 (integer division 15/2=7). So the impl's `pen = 7` is a simplification of `armorPenetration/2` for the case where defense > 15 and armorPenetrationPercent = 0. But when defense <= 15, vanilla returns defense/2 (capped), not 15/2=7. And when armorPenetrationPercent > 0 (from weapon armor pen like Sharpened +12, which passes armorPenetrationPercent?), the value scales with defense.

So the impl `pen = 7` constant is an approximation: correct only when defense > 15 and no percent armor pen. It deviates for low-defense enemies (vanilla would give def/2, less than 7) and ignores interaction with Sharpened. Also vanilla caps: if armorPenetration > defense → defense/2. Actually with ichor 15 > defense → defense/2. So for defense <= 15, pen = floor(defense/2). The impl gives 7 for all. This is a ⚠️/🔀 deviation. 	private void AddBuff_RemoveOldMeleeBuffsOfMatchingType(int type)
	{
		if (!Main.meleeBuff[type])
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < maxBuffs; i++)
		{
			if (buffType[i] != type && Main.meleeBuff[buffType[i]])
			{
				DelBuff(i);
				i--;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddBuff_RemoveOldPetBuffsOfMatchingType(int type)
	{
		if (Main.lightPet[type])
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < maxBuffs; i++)
			{
				if (Main.lightPet[buffType[i]])
				{
					DelBuff(i);
				}
			}
		}
		if (!Main.vanityPet[type])
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < maxBuffs; j++)
		{
			if (Main.vanityPet[buffType[j]])
			{
				DelBuff(j);
			}
		}
	}

	private bool AddBuff_TryUpdatingExistingBuffTime(int type, int time)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < maxBuffs; i++)
		{
			if (buffType[i] != type)
			{
				continue;
			}
			int num = BuffID.Sets.AddBuffTimeAdditivelyToCap[type];
			if (num > 0)
			{
				buffTime[i] += time;
				if (buffTime[i] > num)
				{
					buffTime[i] = num;
				}
			}
			else if (buffTime[i] < time)
			{
				buffTime[i] = time;
			}
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private int AddBuff_DetermineBuffTimeToAdd(int type, int time)
	{
		if (deadCellsPotionStation && BuffID.Sets.BuffTimeIsExtendedByDeadCellsPotionStationBuff[type])

   *  因此 confused = 索敌的水平方向反向（directionY 不反转）。本仓各 AI 的
   *  "TargetClosest"= 从玩家算 facing 的赋值点，统一走 {@link seekDirX} 应用反转；
   *  直接用玩家坐标差算位移向量的族（AI_005 蜂群/AI_013 植物/AI_056 地牢之魂）

  get confused(): boolean { return this.confusedT > 0; }

  seekDirX(player: Player | null): number {
    if (!player) return this.facing;

    // （itemAnimation≈useTime）&& aggro<0（潜行）&& 有效距离 > (双方尺寸和/4+800)
    // && 非 Boss → 保留旧朝向（潜行侧袭不被察觉）；否则正常面向玩家。
    // ★单人下这就是 aggro 的全部消费位（TargetClosest 的多玩家选距在单人退化）
    const agg = (player as unknown as { aggro?: number }).aggro ?? 0;
    if (agg < 0 && player.useTime <= 0 && !this.def.boss) {
      const eff = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) - agg;

Very important finding for checklist C: The aura buffs ARE player buffs in vanilla, granted every 2 ticks via AddBuff (Player.cs:25235-25270):
- ZoneWaterCandle → AddBuff(86, 2) [Water Candle buff id 86]
- ZonePeaceCandle → AddBuff(157, 2)
- ZoneShadowCandle → AddBuff(350, 2)
- HasCampfire → AddBuff(87, 2)
- HasCatBast → AddBuff(215, 2)
- HasStarInBottle → AddBuff(158, 2)
- HasHeartLantern → AddBuff(89, 2)  ← Heart Lantern is buff 89, NOT buff 89... the impl has no HeartLantern BuffType! Impl uses lanternHeal field instead. Hmm, the impl models lantern via `lanternHeal` and `lanternAccum` — equivalent effect but not a buff icon. Vanilla shows a Heart Lantern buff icon. Minor UI gap.
- HasSunflower → AddBuff(146, 2)
- hasBanner → AddBuff(147, 2)

So the impl's buff-proxy modeling for campfire/sunflower/peace candle/cat bast/star in bottle is actually FAITHFUL to vanilla (vanilla also grants these as buffs). Good — the impl comment "原版为 SceneMetrics.HasCampfire 光环…本仓库沿用 Game 每 20tick 扫描写入的持续小时长 buff 表达" is slightly inaccurate in claiming vanilla doesn't model it as a buff, but the effect (lifeRegen++ from HasCampfire check) matches. Note the subtle difference: in vanilla, the Campfire buff 87 icon appears AND SceneMetrics.HasCampfire drives lifeRegen++; the buff itself has no effect. In the impl, the buff drives the heal. Net HP effect identical.

=== Game.ts 9015-9095
  }

  /** contactOnly：伤害源是 NPC **接触**（Player.cs:30880-30970 的 Main.npc[i] 循环）——
   *  荆棘反伤/狼人虚弱只在该分支结算；弹幕命中只带 attacker 做死亡归因，不触发反伤 */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: Enemy, contactOnly = false) {
    const p = this.player;
    if (p.dead) return;
    if (attacker) p.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'npc', name: (attacker.vanillaId != null ? Lang.npcName(attacker.vanillaId) : null) ?? attacker.def.name };
    const ok = p.damage(dmg, srcX, srcY);
    if (ok) {
      // 狼人(155)命中施加虚弱 33（1800t=30s：近战-5.1%伤/速、防-4、移-10%）
      if (attacker?.vanillaId === 155) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Weak, 30);
      // 星星斗篷族受击反击（Player.cs:37826-37870）：3 颗星，出点 x=position.X+Next(-400,400)、
      // y=position.Y-Next(500,800)，速度 = 朝(玩家中心 X 再 ±100 抖动)归一化到 23 速；
      // 弹型按装备 override 件取（manaCloak 723 > beeCloak 724 > starVeil 725 > starCloak 726），
      // 伤害 num18=75、大师 ×3（:37861-37863）/ 专家 ×2（:37864-37866）、击退 5；
      // NewProjectile 尾参 ai1=position.Y（:37870 落点线：星落到该 Y 才触发
      // FallingStar 落地判定）——Arrow 无 ai 槽，登记依赖。
      // ★ Main.masterMode/expertMode 经 mainMasterMode/mainExpertMode 求值
      //   （Main.cs:2760/2762 = Difficulty>=Master/Expert；getGoodWorld 抬档同样生效）
      const accVids = accessoryVanillaIds(p);
      const star = starCloakStarInfo(accVids, p.equipStats.starCloak);
      if (star.active) {
        let starDmg = 75;
        if (mainMasterMode(this.world)) starDmg *= 3;
        else if (mainExpertMode(this.world)) starDmg *= 2;
        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
          const sx = p.x + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 800) - 400);
          const sy = p.y - (500 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 300));
          let n15 = (p.x + p.w / 2) - sx + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 201) - 100);
          let n16 = (p.y + p.h / 2) - sy;
          const n17 = 23 / Math.hypot(n15, n16);
          n15 *= n17; n16 *= n17;
          this.entities.add(new Arrow(sx, sy, n15, n16, starDmg, 5, star.projType, null), 'projectiles');
        }
      }
      // 蜂蜜斗篷/蜂巢族受击反击（Player.cs:37871-37907）：
      //   数量 num19 = 1 + (Next(3)==0) + (Next(3)==0) + (strongBees && Next(3)==0)
      //     （1/2/3/4 只，概率 4/9·4/9·2/9·…，非旧"1-3 均匀"）；
      //   基伤 num20 = strongBees ? 18 : 13（专家 ×1.5/大师 ×2）；
      //   beeType（:12006-12015）strongBees 且 Next(2)==0 → 566（巨蜂），否则 181；
      //   beeDamage（:12017-12025）入参 (int)num20（:37899 截断后）→ 181 +Next(2)、566 +Next(1,4)；
      //   beeKB（:12026-12032）566 → 0.5、181 → 0；出点 = 玩家左上角（position.X/Y）；
      //   初速 = Next(-35,36)*0.02（aiStyle 36 追踪 AI 未建模，以下方瞄敌近似，登记）；
      //   反击后 AddBuff(48 Honey, 300t=5s)（:37906）；原版此处无声效（旧 beeSummon 已删）
      if (p.equipStats.bees) {
        const strong = accVids.includes(3333);
        const roll = beeRetaliateRoll(strong, Math.random);
        const num19 = roll.count;
        // num20 = strongBees ? 18 : 13，再按世界难度缩放（:37884-37891）：
        //   大师 ×2 / 专家 ×1.5（Main.masterMode/expertMode，getGoodWorld 抬档同生效）
        const num20 = mainMasterMode(this.world) ? roll.base * 2
          : mainExpertMode(this.world) ? roll.base * 1.5
            : roll.base;
        for (let k = 0; k < num19; k++) {
          const makeStrong = strong && Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0;
          const beeType = makeStrong ? 566 : 181;
          const beeDmg = makeStrong ? Math.trunc(num20) + 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)
            : Math.trunc(num20) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
          const beeKb = makeStrong ? 0.5 : 0;
          const tgt = this.entities.enemies.find((e) => {
            const en = e as Enemy;
            return !en.dead && Math.abs(en.cx - p.cx) < 500;
          }) as Enemy | undefined;
          const ang = tgt
            ? Math.atan2(tgt.cy - p.cy, tgt.cx - p.cx)
            : -Math.PI / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5);
          const spd = 3.4; // aiStyle 36 追踪档出速占位（真值 ±0.7 抖动由追踪 AI 接管）
          this.entities.add(new Arrow(p.x, p.y, Math.cos(ang) * spd, Math.sin(ang) * spd,
            beeDmg, beeKb, beeType, null), 'projectiles');
        }
        p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Honey, 5);
      }
      // 致死一击：不播受击声，只播死亡声（保证最后听到的音效是死亡）
      if (p.hp <= 0) this.sfx.play('pkilled');
      else this.sfx.play('hurt');
      // 实际扣血 = max(1, dmg - defense*0.5)（在 Player.damage 内）
      const dealt = Math.max(1, dmg - p.defense * 0.5);
      this.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, Math.round(dealt), false, '#FF5050');
      // 荆棘 Buff（1.4.5.6 Player.cs:30910-30944，NPC 接触分支内——contactOnly 门：
      // 弹幕命中不反伤）：thorns=1 → 反弹 num4 × thorns，上限 1000。

  * Internal [NPC ID](NPC_IDs.md "NPC IDs"): **155**

The **Wolf** is a [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode") [enemy](Enemies.md "Enemies") that spawns in the [Snow biome](Snow_biome.md "Snow biome") at [night](Day_and_night_cycle.md "Day and night cycle"). It has the same [AI](AI.md "AI") as the [Unicorn](Unicorn.md "Unicorn"), and attacks by leaping and charging into the player. Like Unicorns, Wolves move at very fast speeds and can jump fairly high, making them one of the hardest enemies to evade. Due to its relatively high defense and health, it is not recommended for early [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode") players to fight these animals. If the player does happen to come across them, it is difficult to outrun them, even with [sprinting accessories](Sprinting_accessories.md "Sprinting accessories"). 

## Tips

  * Since Wolves spawn on the surface, weapons that rain projectiles such as the [Daedalus Stormbow](Daedalus_Stormbow.md "Daedalus Stormbow") can hit them without being stopped by blocks above.
  * The player may dig a hole in the ground 2 tiles wide to stand in and attack Wolves from there, since they will be too wide to fit.
  * Standing on any block/platform at least 8 tiles high prevents Wolves from reaching the player, allowing the player to safely attack without having to dodge.
  * Melee users may find [yoyos](Yoyos.md "Yoyos")**** __(Desktop, Console and Mobile versions) useful, particularly the [Amarok](Amarok.md "Amarok"), which drops from any enemy in a Snow biome and can inflict the [Frostburn](Frostburn.md "Frostburn") [debuff](Debuffs.md "Debuffs"). The [Hel-Fire](Hel-Fire.md "Hel-Fire") works as well if the player has been grinding for it in the [Caverns](Caverns "Caverns") or [The Underworld](The_Underworld.md "The Underworld").
  * Another viable strategy would be to build a large platform at least 15 tiles above the Snow biome; Wolves and other enemies will spawn on top of the platform, and assuming the player has a [mount](Mount "Mount") with a max speed of 46 mph, they can easily outrun them.

## Trivia

  * The Wolf's sprite appears to have small amounts of blood spilled around its mouth, although, as far as its new sprite is concerned, its mouth may simply be ajar.
  * Both the [Unicorn](Unicorn.md "Unicorn") and the Wolf have a turning animation before they begin to run and charge again.

  * The [Bestiary](Bestiary.md "Bestiary") entry for the Wolf: _" Living in the harshest of climates, these savage lupines hunt in the dark of night desperately for sustenance."_

  get aggro(): number {

  get meleeArmorPen(): number {

  critChance(kind: 'melee' | 'ranged' | 'magic'): number {
    const st = this.equipStats;
    let c = kind === 'melee' ? st.critMelee : kind === 'ranged' ? st.critRanged : st.critMagic;
    // R1 药水：暴怒(115)全系暴击 +10（:9890）；醉酒(25)近战暴击 +2（:11515）

    }
    c += this.buffs.wellFedCrit;    // 食物三档全系暴击 +2/3/4（:11525+）
    return c;

39:**Electrified** is a [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode"), post-[Golem](Golem.md "Golem") [debuff](Debuff "Debuff") that causes the player to lose 4 health per second. If the player is moving left/right, they will lose an additional 16 health per second, totaling to a loss of 20 health per second. The damage inflicted by this debuff is capable of killing or severely weakening players relying on constant movement to avoid damage from [enemies](Enemies.md "Enemies"). Using a [hook](Hooks.md "Hooks") or teleporting with a [Rod of Discord](Rod_of_Discord.md "Rod of Discord") will not increase damage. Pressing [`◀ Left`](Game_controls.md "Game controls") or [`▶ Right`](Game_controls.md "Game controls") when unable to move (hooks, [blocks](Blocks.md "Blocks"), other debuffs, etc.) will also increase damage.

33:**Suffocation** is a [debuff](Debuff "Debuff") that is inflicted when the player is submerged in falling [Sand](Sand "Sand"), [Silt](Silt "Silt"), or [Slush](Slush "Slush"), causing them to lose 20 health per second for as long as they are submerged. [Drowning](Drowning.md "Drowning") will not inflict this debuff. Red damage numbers appear as the player loses health, like other debuffs that inflict continuous damage.

33:**Mana Sickness** is a [debuff](Debuff "Debuff") that reduces the player's [magic damage](Magic_damage.md "Magic damage") after they consume any [mana](Mana.md "Mana")-restoring item. Automatically drinking Mana Potions with the [Mana Flower](Mana_Flower.md "Mana Flower") will also cause the debuff. Whenever a mana-restoring item is consumed, the player is inflicted with 5 seconds of Mana Sickness.[1] The percentage of magic damage reduction is proportional to the remaining debuff duration (see Notes for formula), reducing by 1% every 1/5 second; at 5 seconds remaining, the damage reduction is 25%. All mana-restoring items inflict the same length of the debuff; for example, a [Lesser Mana Potion](Lesser_Mana_Potion.md "Lesser Mana Potion") induces the same duration of Mana Sickness as a [Greater Mana Potion](Greater_Mana_Potion.md "Greater Mana Potion"). 
35:****__(Desktop, Console and Mobile versions) If the player consumes additional mana-restoring items while the debuff is active, the duration will be increased by 5 seconds for each item consumed, up to a maximum of 10 seconds (50% damage reduction).[2]

The buff is applied to any players standing within a rectangular area of 170×125[1] tiles centered on the Garden Gnome, similar to the [Heart Lantern](Heart_Lantern.md "Heart Lantern"). Additional Garden Gnomes do not increase luck further. 
67:  * As they are relatively easy to obtain, it can be advantageous to place them wherever Luck comes into play, e.g. [boss](Boss "Boss") arenas and farming locations.

32:Buff tooltip|  _Decreased monster spawn rate_  
43:The Peace Candle buff decreases spawn rates by 23% in the area and decreases the maximum number of simultaneous on-screen enemies by 30%. The buff is applied to any players holding a Peace Candle or standing within a rectangular area of 170×125[1] tiles centered on a Peace Candle. In order to be considered "holding" the candle, it must be in the player's [hotbar](Hotbar.md "Hotbar") and currently selected; merely being in a player's inventory will not suffice. Multiple Candles will not stack with each other, even if one is placed and the other held. Peace Candles can only be placed on [platforms](Platforms.md "Platforms") and flat [furniture](Furniture.md "Furniture") items like [Work Benches](Work_Benches.md "Work Benches") and [Tables](Tables.md "Tables"). 
49:The Peace Candle's effect is the opposite of the [Water Candle](Water_Candle.md "Water Candle"), which increases enemy spawn rate. If a Peace Candle and a Water Candle are active at the same time, they will cancel each other's effects out, resulting in no difference in spawn rate. 
##### Water_Candle
33:Debuff tooltip|  _Increased monster spawn rate_  
44:The Water Candle debuff increases all [enemy](Enemy "Enemy") and [critter](Critter "Critter") spawn rates by 33%, and increases the maximum number of simultaneous on-screen enemies by 50%. The debuff is applied to any players holding or standing within a rectangular area of 170×125[1] tiles centered on a Water Candle. In order to be considered "holding" the candle, it must be in the player's [hotbar](Hotbar.md "Hotbar") and currently selected; merely being in a player's inventory will not suffice. Multiple candles will not stack with each other, even if one is placed and the other held. 
50:The Water Candle's effect is the opposite of the [Peace Candle](Peace_Candle.md "Peace Candle"), which decreases enemy spawn rates. If a Peace Candle and a Water Candle are active at the same time, neither have any effect on spawn rates. Conversely, the Water Candle's effect stacks with that of the [Battle Potion](Battle_Potion.md "Battle Potion").

/** Enemy 侧已实装的 buff 字段(秒)。GAP:30 Bleeding/36 BrokenArmor/44 Frostburn/
 *  137 Slimed/151 SoulDrain/153 ShadowFlame/169 BoneJavelin/183 StardustBleed/

const SUPPORTED: Record<number, string> = {
  24: 'onFireT',     // On Fire!(NPC.cs:92619 lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s)
  20: 'poisonT',     // Poisoned(-12 → 6 HP/s,:92590)
  70: 'venomT',      // Venom(-60 → 30 HP/s,:92706)
  39: 'cursedT',     // Cursed Inferno(onFire2,-48 → 24 HP/s,:92690)
  31: 'confusedT',   // Confused(TargetClosest direction 反转,:78569)
  69: 'ichorT',      // Ichor(防御 -15;repo 近似=armorPen 7,Enemy.ts:4132)
  72: 'midasT',      // Midas(掉钱 ×1.10-1.51,Enemy.ts:4286 rollCoins)
};

type StatusEnemy = { [field: string]: number | undefined };

/** 命中结算侧调用:按投射物型号掷骰并施加 debuff(AddBuff max 合并语义)。
 *  duration 掷骰 Next(durMin, durMax+1) tick → 秒(/60) */
export function applyProjStatus(projId: number | undefined, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
  if (projId === undefined || projId < 0) return;
  const entries = TABLE[String(projId)];
  if (!entries) return;
  for (const en of entries) {
    if (Math.random() >= en.chance) continue;
    const field = SUPPORTED[en.buff];
    if (!field) continue; // 未实装 buff,登记见上
    const ticks = en.durMin + Math.floor(Math.random() * (en.durMax - en.durMin + 1));
    const sec = ticks / 60;
    const cur = enemy[field] ?? 0;
    if (sec > cur) enemy[field] = sec;
  }
}

#### D. 机制性规则

| # | 项 | 原版设定 | 本仓库实现 | 状态 | 差异细节 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | AddBuff 时长合并 | 同类取 max 不缩短(:5179-5190);**例外**:AdditiveToCap={94→600, 383→43200} 叠加封顶(BuffID.cs:34) | ✅ max(Buffs.ts:276-279);**AdditiveToCap 例外未接**(ManaSickness 不可叠加,见 A28) | ⚠️ | 核心规则对,两条例外缺 |
| D2 | 专家/大师 debuff 缩放 | **时长** ×2/×2.5(GameDifficultyData.cs:71 DebuffTimeMultiplier=1/2/2.5),仅作用于 {20,22,23,24,323,30,31,32,33,35,36,39,44,324,46,47,69,70,80}(BuffID.cs:28,Player.cs:5210-5215);**DoT 伤害不随难度缩放**;wiki"Expert +100%/Master +150%" ✓ | **全仓无任何 buff 时长难度缩放**(grep DebuffTimeMultiplier 无果) | ❌ | 清单项"伤害是否缩放"的答案:缩的是**时长**不是伤害,且本仓两者皆无 |
| D3 | 蜂蜜机制 | 浸蜜每 tick AddBuff(48,1800)→恒 30s,离开倒计时;lifeRegen+2=1 HP/s+lifeRegenTime+2(:27436/:18955-18957);**lifeRegen<0 时额外 +4=2 HP/s 对冲并钳回≤0**(:18915-18922);不治中毒 | ✅ 四点全对(Buffs.ts:276/330/353-356;Player.ts:775;Enemy.ts:763 蜂蜜史莱姆 30s;Game.ts:9086 蜂巢饰品 5s) | ✅ | — |

#### 📄 wiki 与源码不符(源码裁决)

1. **Werewolf 流血概率**:wiki Bleeding.md/Werewolf.md 称 1/8(12.5%);1456 源码 `(104\|\|102) && Next(5)==0` = **1/5(20%)**、2700t=45s(Player.cs:22258-22260)。时长 45/90/112.5 与难度倍率自洽,概率以源码为准。
2. **Buffs.md 索引"Calming −17%"**:药水页 −39%/−40% 才对(源码 ×1.65/×0.6),索引过时。
3. **Star in a Bottle**:页 5 mana/s vs 索引 2 MP/s 两说;源码是公式(bonus+10/delay+0.5),以公式为准。
4. **Campfire"+10% 再生"**:源码仅 lifeRegen++(0.5 HP/s),未见 10% 乘区。
5. **Darkness":降低亮度"**:1456 全仓无消费者,纯图标。
6. **Betsys Curse 时长**:Debuffs.md 5s vs Betsy's_Wrath.md 10s 自相矛盾。
7. **Silenced"1.4.5.0 不再影响召唤武器"**:源码 `sItem.mana>0 && silence`(:51383)未见召唤豁免,以源码为准(❓可能实现在别处)。
8. **"Ogre Sped"不存在**:现名 Oozed(OgreSpit 197,moveSpeed/3)。

### 覆盖清单

**共核 88 项**(A 组 32 / B 组 30 / C 组 12 / D 组 7 / E 组 5 + wiki 勘误 8)。
- ✅ 对齐:**41**
- ⚠️ 数值/逻辑偏差:**17**(A6 A13 A22 A26 A28 B8 B9 B27 B30 C5 C8 D1 + 半边项 A1 A2 A4 归差)
- ❌ 未实现:**27**(A5 A7 A9 A11 A12 A14 A16 A21 A23 A24 A25 A30 A31 A32 B14 B16 B19 B20 C9 C10 D2 D4 D5 D7 E1-E4 等)
- 🔀 本地设计:1(B29);📄 wiki 勘误:8;❓:2(C11 C12)
- 结论:记忆所称"原版化+1456 数值已完成"**对已实装的子集成立**(已接的数值抽查几乎全对,含 B5/B6 标签互换、A6/A28 近似均有自查注释),但**覆盖面远未到全表**——玩家侧 debuff 名册约缺 15 种、免疫链双侧全缺、难度时长缩放缺。

### 最需修复 TOP 8

1. **玩家 debuff 名册+来源链(系统性)**:补 Poisoned/Venom/Cursed Inferno/Ichor/Frostburn/Slow/BrokenArmor/Electrified/Silenced/Cursed 等 BuffType,并移植 `StatusFromNPC`(Player.cs:22058-22337)与 `Projectile.StatusPlayer`(:11450+);顺带修 A31(155=Wolf 不该挂 Weak;狼人 104 才是 Bleeding 1/5 45s)。这是本域最大缺口。
2. **难度时长缩放**:`apply` 处接 `DebuffTimeMultiplier`(Classic 1/Expert 2/Master 2.5)× BuffID.Sets.BuffTimeIsExtendedWithGameDifficulty 集合(GameDifficultyData.cs:71)。
3. **buffImmune 双侧**:玩家侧配饰/套装链(Player.cs:14916-15030/15885/15891)+ 免疫清存量(:25699);NPC 侧 NPCID.Sets.DebuffImmunitySets+派生规则(NPC.cs:17752-17767)。
4. **Mana Sickness 双修**:去 `min(0.25,…)` 封顶 + 接 AdditiveToCap(94→600) 叠加,否则峰值 25% vs 原版 50%。
5. **Inferno 光环字段错**:Game.ts:6862 `cursedT` 应为 OnFire3(15 HP/s),现按 Cursed Inferno 吃 24 HP/s。
6. **ichor 穿甲近似收口**:Enemy.ts:4191 定值 7 → `min(15+武器穿甲+def×percent, def)/2`,低防怪高估且不与磨刀联动。
7. **死代码/半接位收尾**:AmmoBox 接 20% 节弹、Hunter 接高亮、Sonar 接显示、Clairvoyance 补 3 项、蛋糕/风筝挖速、Kite/Stinky 授予来源;并清 Buffs.ts:46-48 三条过时注释。
8. **projStatus 缺字段批**:Frostburn/Daybreak/Shadowflame/BetsysCurse/BrokenArmor/Bleeding/SoulDrain/Hemorrhage 数据已在表,补 Enemy 字段即可点亮(数据驱动,成本最低)。
